================================================================================
ANALYSIS NOTES
================================================================================

1. Offsets are extracted from bytecode analysis.
2. Method associations are bytecode-based.
3. Co-occurrence means only that offsets appear in the same detected method.
4. Co-occurrence does NOT prove identical semantic meaning.
5. Modulo-101 groups are mathematical groupings only.
6. No automatic names such as unit, building, coordinate or health are assigned.
